      نظام اختبارات التوظيف الإلكترونية
    </p>

    {/* حقول الإدخال */}
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <Input
          placeholder="اسم المستخدم"
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleLogin()}
          className={clsx(
            theme.input.base,
            "!mb-0 text-center tracking-wide focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/20"
          )}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <Input
          type="password"
          placeholder="كلمة المرور"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleLogin()}
          className={clsx(
            theme.input.base,
            "!mb-0 text-center tracking-widest focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/20"
          )}
        />
      </div>

      {/* زر تسجيل الدخول */}
      <Button 
        className="w-full py-2.5 shadow-md shadow-primary/20 font-semibold" 
        onClick={handleLogin} 
        disabled={loading}
      >
        {loading ? (
          <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" />
            جاري الدخول...
          </span>
        ) : (
          'تسجيل الدخول'
        )}
      </Button>
    </div>

    {/* رسالة الخطأ */}
    {error && (
      <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:border-red-900/50 dark:text-red-400 text-xs text-center font-medium animate-shake">
        {error}
      </div>
    )}
  </div>
</div>
  )
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import clsx from 'clsx'
import { UserPen, RefreshCcw, UserX } from 'lucide-react'

import { applicantsApi } from '../../api/adminApi'
import { extractErrorMessage } from '../../api/axiosClient'
import Button from '../../components/Button'
import Input from '../../components/Input'
import StatusBadge from '../../components/StatusBadge'
import AccessCodeModal from '../../modals/AccessCodeModal'
import ApplicantModal from '../../modals/ApplicantModal'
import { downloadBlob } from '../../utils/helpers'
import { ConfirmDialog,FormField,Select } from '../../components/common/index'
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme'

export default function ApplicantsTab({ jobModels = [], onCreated }) {
  const [applicants, setApplicants] = useState([])
  const [jobFilter, setJobFilter] = useState('')
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  // حالات النوافذ المنبثقة
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedApplicant, setSelectedApplicant] = useState(null)
  const [createdCodeData, setCreatedCodeData] = useState(null)

  // حالة التحكم في نافذة تأكيد تجديد الكود
  const [resetConfirmApplicant, setResetConfirmApplicant] = useState(null)

  const loadApplicants = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const { data } = await applicantsApi.list(jobFilter || undefined)
      setApplicants(data || [])
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر تحميل قائمة المتقدمين'))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [jobFilter])

  useEffect(() => {
    loadApplicants()
  }, [loadApplicants])

  function handleOpenCreate() {
    setSelectedApplicant(null)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  function handleOpenEdit(applicant) {
    setSelectedApplicant(applicant)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  async function handleDelete(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المتقدم وكل بياناته ورسائل الاختبار الخاصة به؟')) return
    try {
      await applicantsApi.remove(id)
      loadApplicants()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر حذف المتقدم'))
    }
  }

  async function handleResetExam(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('هل أنت متأكد من إعادة تعيين حالة الاختبار لهذا المتقدم؟')) return
    try {
      await applicantsApi.resetExam(id)
      loadApplicants()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر حذف المتقدم'))
    }
  }

  function handleOpenResetConfirm(applicant) {
    setResetConfirmApplicant(applicant)
  }

  async function handleConfirmResetCode() {
    if (!resetConfirmApplicant) return
    try {
      const { data } = await applicantsApi.resetCode(resetConfirmApplicant.id)
      setCreatedCodeData({
        full_name: resetConfirmApplicant.full_name,
        access_code: data.access_code,
      })
      setResetConfirmApplicant(null)
      loadApplicants()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر إعادة توليد كود الدخول'))
    }
  }

  function handleModalSuccess(createdData) {
    loadApplicants()
    onCreated?.()
    if (createdData && createdData.access_code) {
      setCreatedCodeData(createdData)
    }
  }

  function copyToClipboard(text) {
    if (!text) return
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
    alert('تم نسخ كود الدخول إلى الحافظة')
  }

  // فلترة حسب البحث بالاسم أو الرقم القومي أو الكود
  const filteredApplicants = applicants.filter((a) => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase()
    if (!q) return true
    return (
      a.full_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.national_id?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.access_code?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    )
  })

  function handleExportCsv() {
    if (filteredApplicants.length === 0) {
      alert('لا توجد بيانات للتصدير')
      return
    }

    const headers = ['اسم المتقدم', 'كود الدخول', 'الرقم القومي', 'الوظيفة']
    
    const rows = filteredApplicants.map((a) => [
      `"${(a.full_name || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
      `"${(a.access_code || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
      `"${(a.national_id || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
      `"${(a.job_model_name || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
    ])

    const csvContent = '\uFEFF' + [headers.join(','), ...rows.map((r) => r.join(','))].join('\n')

    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' })
    const fileName = `applicants_export_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`
    downloadBlob(blob, fileName)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* الشريط العلوي للتحكم والفلترة */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4 flex-wrap">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap flex-1">
          <FormField>
            <Input
              placeholder="بحث بالاسم أو الرقم القومي..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className={clsx(theme.input.base)}
            />
          </FormField>
          <FormField>
            <Select options={jobModels.map((job) => ({
                value: job.id,
                label: job.name,
              }))}
            placeholder='كل الوظائف'
            value={jobFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setJobFilter(e.target.value)}
            />
          </FormField>
          
          
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleExportCsv} className={clsx(theme.button.base,theme.button.secondary)}>
            📄 تصدير CSV
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={handleOpenCreate} className={clsx(theme.button.base,theme.button.primary)}>+ إضافة متقدم جديد</Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="text-danger text-sm mb-3">{error}</p>}

      {/* جدول عرض المتقدمين */}
      <div className="card overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-primary bg-slate-50 border-b border-gray-200">
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الاسم</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الرقم القومي</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الوظيفة</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">كود الدخول</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الحالة</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">إجراءات</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {filteredApplicants.map((a) => (
              <tr key={a.id} className="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-slate-50/50">
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">{a.full_name}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-gray-600">{a.national_id}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-gray-600">{a.job_model_name}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="font-mono font-bold text-primary bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                      {a.access_code}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => copyToClipboard(a.access_code)}
                      title="نسخ الكود"
                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-primary text-xs"
                    >
                      📋
                    </button>
                    

                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleOpenResetConfirm(a)}
                      className="text-red-400 hover:text-blue-500 text-xs hover:background-blue-500 p-1 rounded"
                      title="إعادة توليد كود دخول جديد"
                    >
                      <RefreshCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                  
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleResetExam(a.id)}
                      className="text-red-400 hover:text-blue-500 text-xs hover:background-blue-500 p-1 rounded"
                      title="إعادة تعيين حالة الاختبار"
                    >
                      🔄
                    </button>
                  <StatusBadge status={a.status} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(a)}
                      className={clsx(theme.button.base, theme.button.primary, 'flex items-center gap-1.5')}
                    >
                      <UserPen className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDelete(a.id)}
                      className={clsx(theme.button.base, theme.button.danger, 'flex items-center gap-1.5')}
                    >
                      <UserX className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>

        {!loading && filteredApplicants.length === 0 && (
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-8">
            {searchQuery || jobFilter ? 'لا توجد نتائج تطابق خيارات البحث.' : 'لا يوجد متقدمون بعد.'}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* النوافذ المنبثقة */}
      <ApplicantModal
        open={modalOpen}
        initialData={selectedApplicant}
        jobModels={jobModels}
        onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
        onSuccess={handleModalSuccess}
      />

      <AccessCodeModal
        open={Boolean(createdCodeData)}
        applicantData={createdCodeData}
        onClose={() => setCreatedCodeData(null)}
      />

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={Boolean(resetConfirmApplicant)}
        onClose={() => setResetConfirmApplicant(null)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmResetCode}
        title="تأكيد إعادة توليد كود الدخول"
        message={`هل أنت متأكد من إعادة إنشاء كود دخول جديد للمتقدم (${resetConfirmApplicant?.full_name})؟ سيتم إبطال الكود القديم.`}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { jobModelsApi } from '../../api/adminApi'
import { extractErrorMessage } from '../../api/axiosClient'
import Button from '../../components/Button'
import JobModelModal from '../../modals/JobModelModal'

export default function JobModelsTab({ jobModels, onRefresh }) {
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedModel, setSelectedModel] = useState(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    onRefresh()
  }, []) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  function handleOpenCreate() {
    setSelectedModel(null)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  function handleOpenEdit(model) {
    setSelectedModel(model)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  async function handleDelete(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا النموذج؟ قد يترتب على ذلك حذف الأسئلة المرتبطة به.')) return
    setError('')
    try {
      await jobModelsApi.remove(id)
      onRefresh()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر حذف نموذج الوظيفة'))
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
  {/* الشريط العلوي */}
  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
    <div>
      <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">
        نماذج الوظائف
      </h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
        إدارة نماذج الاختبارات والأوقات المحددة لكل وظيفة
      </p>
    </div>
    <Button onClick={handleOpenCreate} className="shadow-sm">
      + إضافة نموذج جديد
    </Button>
  </div>

  {error && (
    <div className="p-3 mb-4 text-xs rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-950/30 dark:border-red-800/50 dark:text-red-400">
      {error}
    </div>
  )}

  {/* قائمة الكروت */}
  <div className="space-y-3">
    {jobModels.map((jm) => (
      <div
        key={jm.id}
        className="group bg-white dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/60 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 transition-all duration-200 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600 hover:shadow-sm"
      >
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
            <strong className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">
              {jm.name}
            </strong>

            {/* شارة المدة */}
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200/50 dark:border-slate-600/50">
              ⏱️ {jm.duration_minutes} دقيقة
            </span>

            {/* شارة عدد الأسئلة (إن وجدت) */}
            {jm.count_questions !== undefined && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200/50 dark:border-slate-600/50">
                📝 {jm.count_questions} أسئلة
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {jm.description && (
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
              {jm.description}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* أزرار الإجراءات */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 opacity-90 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
          <button
            onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(jm)}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary dark:text-primary-light hover:bg-primary/10 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            تعديل
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => handleDelete(jm.id)}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            حذف
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    ))}

    {jobModels.length === 0 && (
      <div className="text-center py-10 border border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl bg-slate-50/50 dark:bg-slate-900/40">
        <span className="text-2xl block mb-2">📋</span>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          لا توجد نماذج وظائف بعد.
        </p>
      </div>
    )}
  </div>

  <JobModelModal
    open={modalOpen}
    initialData={selectedModel}
    onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
    onSuccess={onRefresh}
  />
</div>
  )
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import clsx from 'clsx'
import { Plus, Trash2, HelpCircle, Edit3, Printer } from 'lucide-react'
import { pdf } from '@react-pdf/renderer'

import { questionsApi } from '../../api/adminApi'
import { extractErrorMessage } from '../../api/axiosClient'
import { Select, IconButton, FormField } from '../../components/common/index'
import AddQuestionModal from '../../modals/AddQuestionModal'
import { QuestionPdfDocument } from '../../components/pdf/QuestionPdfDocument'
import { 
  QUESTION_TYPE_LABELS, 
  QUESTION_TYPE_ICONS, 
  QUESTION_TYPE_COLORS 
} from '../../constants/index'
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme'

function QuestionCard({ question, onEdit, onDelete, onPrint, isExporting }) {
  const qType = question?.q_type
  const typeLabel = QUESTION_TYPE_LABELS[qType] || qType
  const typeIcon = QUESTION_TYPE_ICONS[qType] || '❓'
  const typeColors = QUESTION_TYPE_COLORS[qType] || {
    badge: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300',
    border: 'border-gray-200/80',
  }

  return (
    <div className={clsx(
      'group bg-white border rounded-xl p-4 transition-all duration-200 hover:shadow-sm',
      typeColors.border || 'border-gray-200/80'
    )}>
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <strong className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 leading-snug">
              {question.question_text}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
            <span>
              الدرجة: <strong className="text-slate-700">{question.points}</strong>
            </span>
            <span className="text-gray-300">•</span>
            <span>
              الترتيب: <strong className="text-slate-700">{question.order_index}</strong>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* أزرار الإجراءات */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <span className={clsx(
              'text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-1.5',
              typeColors.badge
            )}>
              <span>{typeIcon}</span>
              <span>{typeLabel}</span>
            </span>

          {/* زر طباعة هذا السؤال فقط */}
          <IconButton 
            icon={Printer} 
            label="طباعة السؤال PDF" 
            variant="solid" 
            size="md" 
            disabled={isExporting}
            onClick={() => onPrint(question)}
            className={clsx(
                theme?.button?.base || 'px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100',
                'flex items-center gap-1'
              )}
          />

          <IconButton 
            icon={Edit3} 
            label="تعديل السؤال" 
            variant="solid" 
            size="md" 
            onClick={() => onEdit(question)}
            className={clsx(
                theme?.button?.base || 'px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                theme?.button?.primary || 'text-primary hover:bg-primary/10',
                'flex items-center gap-1'
              )}
          />

          <IconButton 
            icon={Trash2} 
            label="حذف السؤال" 
            variant="solid" 
            size="md" 
            onClick={() => onDelete(question.id)}
            className={clsx(
                theme?.button?.base || 'px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                theme?.button?.danger || 'text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50',
                'flex items-center gap-1'
              )}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function QuestionsTab({ jobModels = [] }) {
  const [jobModelId, setJobModelId] = useState('')
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState([])
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedQuestion, setSelectedQuestion] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  // الحصول على اسم النموذج الحالي لاستخدامه في عنوان الـ PDF
  const currentJobModelName = jobModels.find((jm) => String(jm.id) === String(jobModelId))?.name || 'نموذج الوظيفة'

  const loadQuestions = useCallback(async (id) => {
    if (!id) {
      setQuestions([])
      return
    }

    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const { data } = await questionsApi.list(id)
      setQuestions(data || [])
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر تحميل قائمة الأسئلة'))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    loadQuestions(jobModelId)
  }, [jobModelId, loadQuestions])

  // دالة التعامل مع التصدير إلى PDF
  const handleExportPdf = async (questionsToExport, customFileName) => {
    if (!questionsToExport || questionsToExport.length === 0) return

    try {
      setIsExporting(true)
      const blob = await pdf(
        <QuestionPdfDocument 
          questions={questionsToExport} 
          jobTitle={currentJobModelName} 
        />
      ).toBlob()

      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
      const link = document.createElement('a')
      link.href = url;
      link.download = customFileName || `إجابات_${currentJobModelName}.pdf`
      link.click()
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('PDF Export Error:', err)
      setError('حدث خطأ أثناء تصدير ملف الـ PDF')
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false)
    }
  }

  function handleOpenCreate() {
    setSelectedQuestion(null)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  function handleOpenEdit(question) {
    setSelectedQuestion(question)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  async function handleDelete(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا السؤال؟')) return
    try {
      await questionsApi.remove(id)
      loadQuestions(jobModelId)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(extractErrorMessage(err, 'تعذر حذف السؤال'))
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* عناصر التحكم العلوية */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3 mb-5">
        <div className="w-full sm:w-72">
          <FormField>
            <Select
              value={jobModelId}
              onChange={(e) => setJobModelId(e.target.value)}
              className="input-field w-full"
              placeholder="اختر نموذج الوظيفة..."
              options={jobModels.map((jm) => ({
                value: jm.id,
                label: jm.name,
              }))}
            />
          </FormField>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* زر طباعة وتنزيل كل الأسئلة للنموذج المختار */}
          {questions.length > 0 && (
            <IconButton 
              icon={Printer} 
              label={isExporting ? "جاري التصدير..." : "طباعة كل الأسئلة (PDF)"} 
              variant="solid" 
              size="md" 
              onClick={() => handleExportPdf(questions, `أسئلة_وإجابات_${currentJobModelName}.pdf`)}
              disabled={isExporting || loading}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white"
            />
          )}

          <IconButton 
            icon={Plus} 
            label="إضافة" 
            variant="solid" 
            size="md" 
            onClick={handleOpenCreate}
            disabled={!jobModelId}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="text-danger text-sm mb-4">{error}</p>}

      {/* قائمة الأسئلة */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {questions.map((q) => (
          <QuestionCard
            key={q.id}
            question={q}
            onEdit={handleOpenEdit}
            onDelete={handleDelete}
            onPrint={(questionToPrint) => handleExportPdf([questionToPrint], `سؤال_${questionToPrint.id}.pdf`)}
            isExporting={isExporting}
          />
        ))}

        {/* حالات فارغة */}
        {loading && (
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-8">جاري تحميل الأسئلة...</p>
        )}

        {!loading && jobModelId && questions.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-10 border border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl bg-slate-50/50">
            <HelpCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">لا توجد أسئلة لهذا النموذج بعد.</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {!jobModelId && (
          <div className="text-center py-12 border border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl bg-slate-50/30">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">اختر نموذج وظيفة من القائمة أعلاه لعرض أسئلته.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* مودال إضافة/تعديل السؤال الموحد */}
      <AddQuestionModal
        open={modalOpen}
        initialData={selectedQuestion}
        jobModelId={jobModelId}
        jobModels={jobModels}
        onClose={() => {
          setModalOpen(false)
          setSelectedQuestion(null)
        }}
        onCreated={() => loadQuestions(jobModelId)}
        onSuccess={() => loadQuestions(jobModelId)}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

===== Src: C:\Users\sinaiwater\Desktop\Docker\project\project\frontend\src\pages\admin\ResultsTab.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { applicantsApi } from '../../api/adminApi'
import StatusBadge from '../../components/StatusBadge'
import { downloadBlob } from '../../utils/helpers'

export default function ResultsTab({ jobModels }) {
  const [applicants, setApplicants] = useState([])
  const [jobFilter, setJobFilter] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function loadResults() {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const { data } = await applicantsApi.list(jobFilter || undefined)
      setApplicants(data)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadResults()
  }, [jobFilter]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  async function handleDelete(id) {
    if (!window.confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المتقدم وكل بياناته؟')) return
    await applicantsApi.remove(id)
    loadResults()
  }

  async function handleDownloadPdf(applicant) {
    const { data } = await applicantsApi.downloadPdf(applicant.id)
    downloadBlob(data, `exam_result_${applicant.full_name}.pdf`)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex gap-2.5 mb-4 flex-wrap">
        <select value={jobFilter} onChange={(e) => setJobFilter(e.target.value)} className="input-field w-auto">
          <option value="">كل الوظائف</option>
          {jobModels.map((jm) => (
            <option key={jm.id} value={jm.id}>
              {jm.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <button onClick={loadResults} className="btn-primary">
          تحديث
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="card overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-primary bg-slate-50">
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الاسم</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الرقم القومي</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الوظيفة</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الحالة</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">الدرجة</th>
              <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5">إجراءات</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {applicants.map((a) => (
              <tr key={a.id} className="border-t border-gray-100">
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{a.full_name}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{a.national_id}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{a.job_model_name}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                  <StatusBadge status={a.status} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                  {a.status === 'finished' ? `${a.total_score} / ${a.max_score}` : '-'}
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2.5 space-x-2 space-x-reverse">
                  {a.status === 'finished' && (
                    <button onClick={() => handleDownloadPdf(a)} className="text-primary text-xs hover:underline">
                      تحميل PDF
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => handleDelete(a.id)} className="text-danger text-xs hover:underline">
                    حذف
                  </button>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {!loading && applicants.length === 0 && (
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-8">لا يوجد متقدمون بعد.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== Src: C:\Users\sinaiwater\Desktop\Docker\project\project\frontend\src\stores\authStore.js =====
import { create } from 'zustand'
import { authApi } from '../api/authApi'
import { extractErrorMessage } from '../api/axiosClient'
import { db } from '../hooks/db' // 1. استيراد ملف الداتا بيز

export const useAuthStore = create((set, get) => ({
  admin: null,
  isAuthenticated: false,
  isLoading: true,

  init: async () => {
    try {
      // قراءة التوكن من Dexie IndexedDB
      const tokenItem = await db.store.get('access_token')
      const token = tokenItem?.value

      if (!token) {
        set({ isLoading: false })
        return
      }

      const { data } = await authApi.me()
      set({ admin: data, isAuthenticated: true, isLoading: false })
    } catch {
      // في حالة الخطأ أو انتهاء الجلسة، نمسح التوكن من Dexie
      await db.store.delete('access_token')
      set({ admin: null, isAuthenticated: false, isLoading: false })
    }
  },

  login: async (username, password) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await authApi.login(username, password)

      // حفظ التوكنات والبيانات في Dexie بدلاً من localStorage
      await Promise.all([
        db.store.put({ key: 'token_type', value: data.token_type }),
        db.store.put({ key: 'access_token', value: data.access_token }),
        db.store.put({ key: 'refresh_token', value: data.refresh_token }),
        db.store.put({ key: 'user_id', value: data.user_id }),
        db.store.put({ key: 'full_name', value: data.full_name }),
      ])

      set({ admin: data.user_id, isAuthenticated: true })
      return { success: true }
    } catch (error) {
      return { success: false, message: extractErrorMessage(error, 'خطأ في تسجيل الدخول') }
    }
  },

  logout: async () => {
    try {
      await authApi.logout()
    } catch {
      // تجاهل الخطأ
    }

    // مسح كافة التوكنات الخاصة بالـ Auth من Dexie
    await Promise.all([
      db.store.delete('token_type'),
      db.store.delete('access_token'),
      db.store.delete('refresh_token'),
      db.store.delete('user_id'),
      db.store.delete('full_name'),
    ])

    set({ admin: null, isAuthenticated: false })
  },
}))

===== Src: C:\Users\sinaiwater\Desktop\Docker\project\project\frontend\src\stores\examStore.js =====
import { create } from 'zustand'
import { persist, createJSONStorage } from 'zustand/middleware'
import { db } from '../hooks/db'

import { examApi } from '../api/examApi'
import { extractErrorMessage } from '../api/axiosClient'
import { EXAM_STORAGE_KEY } from '../constants'

let isSyncing = false // مانع التداخل (Race Condition Guard)

const dexieStorage = {
  getItem: async (name) => {
    const item = await db.store.get(name)
    return item ? item.value : null
  },
  setItem: async (name, value) => {
    await db.store.put({ key: name, value })
  },
  removeItem: async (name) => {
    await db.store.delete(name)
  },
}

export const useExamStore = create(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      token: null,
      user: null,
      duration_minutes: 0,
      count_questions: 0,
      questions: [],
      currentIndex: 0,
      answers: {},
      pendingQueue: [],
      syncStatus: 'idle',
      finished: false,
      finishResult: null,
      toastMessage: null,

      loginWithCode: async (accessCode) => {
        const state = get()

        // 1. حماية النموذج العشوائي: لو كان نفس المستخدم مسجل مسبقاً والامتحان لم ينتهِ، لا نطلب الأسئلة مجدداً
        if (
          state.token &&
          state.user &&
          (state.user.access_code === accessCode || state.user.code === accessCode) &&
          !state.finished &&
          state.questions.length > 0
        ) {
          // استكمال الجلسة الحالية المحفوظة محلياً دون مسح الأسئلة العشوائية
          return { success: true, resumed: true }
        }

        try {
          // 2. طلب نموذج عشوائي جديد من API فقط للمستخدم الجديد/المرة الأولى
          const { data } = await examApi.login(accessCode)

          const savedAnswers = {}
          if (Array.isArray(data.questions)) {
            data.questions.forEach((q) => {
              if (q.saved_answer) savedAnswers[q.id] = q.saved_answer
            })
          }

          set({
            token: data.token,
            user: data.user,
            duration_minutes: data.duration_minutes || 0,
            count_questions: data.job_model_count_questions || data.questions?.length || 0,
            questions: data.questions || [],
            answers: savedAnswers,
            pendingQueue: [],
            currentIndex: 0,
            finished: false,
            finishResult: null,
          })
          return { success: true, resumed: false }
        } catch (error) {
          return { success: false, message: extractErrorMessage(error, 'كود الدخول غير صحيح') }
        }
      },

      setAnswer: async (questionId, answerData) => {
        const { user } = get()
        const userCode = user?.access_code || user?.code || user?.id || 'unknown'

        // 1. التحديث في الـ State الحالية
        set((state) => ({
          answers: { ...state.answers, [questionId]: answerData },
          pendingQueue: [
            ...state.pendingQueue.filter((item) => item.question_id !== questionId),
            { question_id: questionId, answer_data: answerData },
          ],
        }))
        // 2. أرشفة دائمية في Dexie (Permanent Local Backup) لمنع ضياع الإجابة نهائياً
        try {
          await db.answers_archive.put({
            user_code: userCode,
            question_id: questionId,
            answer_data: answerData,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            synced: false,
          })
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Failed to save to backup archive:', e)
        }

        // 3. تشغيل المزامنة مع Server
        get().syncNow()
      },

      goToQuestion: (index) => {
        const { questions } = get()
        if (index < 0 || index >= questions.length) return
        set({ currentIndex: index })
      },

      syncNow: async () => {
        const { pendingQueue } = get()
        
        if (isSyncing || pendingQueue.length === 0) return

        isSyncing = true
        set({ syncStatus: 'syncing' })

        const batch = [...pendingQueue]
        try {
          const { data } = await examApi.saveBatch(batch)
          const succeededIds = new Set(
            (data.results || []).filter((r) => r.success).map((r) => r.question_id)
          )

          // تحديث حالة الأرشيف المحلي كـ Synced داخل Transaction سريعة بدلاً من الحلقات المتسلسلة
          const { user } = get()
          const userCode = user?.access_code || user?.code || user?.id || 'unknown'
          
          if (succeededIds.size > 0) {
            await db.transaction('rw', db.answers_archive, async () => {
              for (const id of succeededIds) {
                const record = await db.answers_archive
                  .where('[user_code+question_id]')
                  .equals([userCode, id])
                  .first()
                if (record) {
                  await db.answers_archive.update(record.id, { synced: true })
                }
              }
            })
          }

          set((state) => ({
            pendingQueue: state.pendingQueue.filter((item) => !succeededIds.has(item.question_id)),
            syncStatus: succeededIds.size === batch.length ? 'ok' : 'error',
          }))
        } catch {
          set({ syncStatus: 'offline' })
        } finally {
          isSyncing = false
        }
      },

      logViolation: async (violationType, details) => {
        // 🟢 1. إظهار الـ Toast للمستخدم
        set({ toastMessage: details })

        // 🟢 2. إخفاء الـ Toast تلقائياً بعد 4 ثوانٍ
        setTimeout(() => {
          set({ toastMessage: null })
        }, 4000)

        // 3. محاولة إرسال المخالفة للسيرفر أو حفظها أوفلاين
        try {
          await examApi.logViolation(violationType, details)
        } catch (error) {
          try {
            await db.offline_violations.add({
              violation_type: violationType,
              details: details,
              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
              synced: false,
            })
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Failed to save violation offline:', e)
          }
        }
      },

      dismissToast: () => set({ toastMessage: null }),

      finishExam: async () => {
        const { pendingQueue } = get()
        if (pendingQueue.length > 0) {
          try {
            await examApi.saveBatch(pendingQueue)
          } catch {
            // اعتماداً على الأرشيف والـ local database
          }
        }
        try {
          const { data } = await examApi.finish()
          const finalResult = data.result

          // تفريغ الـ State النشطة بدون مسح الأرشيف المحلي للمتقدمين
          get().reset()

          set({ finished: true, finishResult: finalResult })
          return { success: true }
        } catch (error) {
          return { success: false, message: extractErrorMessage(error, 'تعذر تسليم الاختبار') }
        }
      },

      reset: () => {
        set({
          token: null,
          user: null,
          duration_minutes: 0,
          count_questions: 0,
          questions: [],
          currentIndex: 0,
          answers: {},
          pendingQueue: [],
          syncStatus: 'idle',
          finished: false,
          finishResult: null,
        })
      },
    }),
    {
      name: EXAM_STORAGE_KEY,
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => dexieStorage),
      // تضمين كلاً من count_questions و duration_minutes للحفظ المستمر مع Refresh
      partialize: (state) => ({
        token: state.token,
        user: state.user,
        duration_minutes: state.duration_minutes,
        count_questions: state.count_questions,
        questions: state.questions,
        answers: state.answers,
        pendingQueue: state.pendingQueue,
        currentIndex: state.currentIndex,
        finished: state.finished,
        finishResult: state.finishResult,
      }),
    }
  )
)


===== Src: C:\Users\sinaiwater\Desktop\Docker\project\project\frontend\src\utils\helpers.js =====
export function formatDateTime(value) {
  if (!value) return '-'
  try {
    const date = new Date(value.endsWith?.('Z') ? value : `${value}Z`)
    return date.toLocaleString('ar-EG', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' })
  } catch {
    return value
  }
}

export function truncate(text, maxLength = 60) {
  if (!text) return ''
  return text.length > maxLength ? `${text.slice(0, maxLength - 3)}...` : text
}

export function downloadBlob(blob, filename) {
  const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = filename
  document.body.appendChild(link)
  link.click()
  link.remove()
  window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom'

import RequireAdmin from './components/RequireAdmin'
import ExamPage from './pages/ExamPage'
import LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage'
import ResultPage from './pages/ResultPage'
import AdminDashboard from './pages/admin/AdminDashboard'
import AdminLoginPage from './pages/admin/AdminLoginPage'
import { useAuthStore } from './stores/authStore'

export default function App() {
  const init = useAuthStore((s) => s.init)

  useEffect(() => {
    init()
  }, [init])

  return (
    <BrowserRouter 
        future={{ 
          v7_startTransition: true, 
          v7_relativeSplatPath: true // 👈 أضف هذا السطر لحل التحذير الجديد
        }}
      >
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<LoginPage />} />
        <Route path="/exam" element={<ExamPage />} />
        <Route path="/result" element={<ResultPage />} />

        <Route path="/admin/login" element={<AdminLoginPage />} />
        <Route
          path="/admin"
          element={
            <RequireAdmin>
              <AdminDashboard />
            </RequireAdmin>
          }
        />

        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}

===== Src: C:\Users\sinaiwater\Desktop\Docker\project\project\frontend\vite.config.js =====
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { VitePWA } from 'vite-plugin-pwa'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    react(),
    VitePWA({
      registerType: 'autoUpdate',
      includeAssets: ['favicon.ico', 'apple-touch-icon.png', 'masked-icon.svg', 'assets/*.png'],
      workbox: {
        maximumFileSizeToCacheInBytes: 5 * 1024 * 1024,
      },
      manifest: {
        name: 'نظام إدارة الاختبارات والمهام',
        short_name: 'نظام الاختبارات',
        description: 'نظام متكامل لإدارة وتنفيذ الاختبارات الإلكترونية والتنبيهات المباشرة',
        theme_color: '#2563eb',
        background_color: '#ffffff',
        display: 'standalone',
        orientation: 'portrait',
        dir: 'rtl',
        lang: 'ar',
        icons: [
          {
            src: '/assets/pwa-192x192.png',
            sizes: '192x192',
            type: 'image/png'
          },
          {
            src: '/assets/pwa-512x512.png',
            sizes: '512x512',
            type: 'image/png'
          },
          {
            src: '/assets/pwa-512x512.png',
            sizes: '512x512',
            type: 'image/png',
            purpose: 'any maskable'
          }
        ]
      }
    })
  ],
  build: {
    chunkSizeWarningLimit: 2000,
    rollupOptions: {
      output: {
        manualChunks(id) {
          if (id.includes('node_modules')) {
            // 1. مفصولات PDF (حجمها ضخم جداً)
            if (id.includes('@react-pdf/renderer')) {
              return 'vendor-pdf';
            }
            // 2. الجداول المتقدمة
            if (id.includes('handsontable') || id.includes('@handsontable/react')) {
              return 'vendor-handsontable';
            }
            // 3. محرر النصوص Tiptap والمحررات
            if (id.includes('@tiptap') || id.includes('@uiw/react-md-editor')) {
              return 'vendor-editors';
            }
            // 4. المعادلات الرياضيات والرموز
            if (id.includes('katex')) {
              return 'vendor-math';
            }
            // 5. الأيقونات
            if (id.includes('lucide-react')) {
              return 'vendor-icons';
            }
            // 6. النواة الأساسية لـ React والـ Router
            if (id.includes('react') || id.includes('react-dom') || id.includes('react-router-dom')) {
              return 'vendor-core';
            }
          }
        },
      },
    },
  },
  server: {
    port: 5050,
    host: true,
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: process.env.VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET || 'http://localhost:5051',
        changeOrigin: true,
        ws: true, // 🟢 تم تمكين دعم الـ WebSocket عبر الـ Proxy
      },
      '/uploads': {
        target: process.env.VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET || 'http://localhost:5051',
        changeOrigin: true,
      },
    },
  },
  preview: {
    port: 5050,
    host: true,
  },
})
